1001 advanced proofs

Theorem 214.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any segmentation module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 567. The projective resolution has length at most 832 with Betti numbers bounded by 4.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 567 is immediate from the convergence analysis property. For the inductive step, we decompose the segmentation structure using the convergence analysis theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 832. Each subproblem has a solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 4. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.

This completes the proof. QED.
